([patrickleehua/easily-skills](https://github.com/patrickleehua/easily-skills/tree/HEAD/media-download-bilibili) · ★2)
- **bili-download**: 当用户要下载、备份、归档、保存或留存 B站视频到本地时触发——尤其涉及某 UP 主的合集、系列、充电或付费课程,或给出 space.bilibili.com 主页链接 / 视频链接 / BV号。即使用户没明说"下载",只要提到把 B站内容存本地、离线看、怕被删/下架想备份,也应触发。 ([Langni/bilibili-collection-downloader](https://github.com/Langni/bilibili-collection-downloader/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **bili-following-cleanup**: Review and clean up a Bilibili (B站) following list end-to-end — read every follow, fetch each up主's recent videos to infer their content niche, classify accounts into unfollow categories with priority, render a local HTML picker, then batch-unfollow through the B站 API with risk-control retries. Use when the user wants to 整理/清理 B站 关注列表, asks "这个 up 是做什么的", or wants to 批量取关 / unfollow Bilibili accounts. ([nothingness0db/bili-following-cleanup](https://github.com/nothingness0db/bili-following-cleanup/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **bili-following-latest**: Run the AI博主爬取 project workflow that downloads the latest Bilibili videos for Feishu Base creators marked 是否持续跟踪=true, writes successful downloads to the Feishu 视频 table, and writes a 爬取任务日志 record. Use when the user asks to crawl/download/update tracked Bilibili creators, 最新三条视频, 持续跟踪博主, or rerun the current project's Feishu+Bilibili batch pipeline. ([dragon-hh/ai-boshu-crawler](https://github.com/dragon-hh/ai-boshu-crawler/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/bili-following-latest) · ★107)
- **bili-hardcore**: B站硬核会员自动答题。Use when the user wants to complete bilibili 硬核会员试炼/六级答题、查询答题得分，或提到 bili-hardcore / 硬核会员 / B站答题 / 哔哩哔哩答题。zero-config，agent 自身作答，无需 LLM API Key。 ([Karben233/bili-hardcore](https://github.com/Karben233/bili-hardcore/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/bili-hardcore) · ★2.4k · 2 copies)
